10 月通胀数据点评：能源链主导上游涨价，低位行业延续修复

 魏争 分析师  沈夏宜 分析师

请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 2 
0.7%，指向工业品价格仍处上行区间，上涨速度出现放缓。结构上看，一
是输入性能源价格继续强化，化工链价格上升。 10 月中东局势扰动仍然
较强，国际能源价格升至高位，输入性价格继续传导。石油和天然气开采
业价格同比升至 35.0%（上月 24.5%），石油煤炭加工业升至 29.9%（上
月 21.6%），化学原料制品制造业升至 16.4%（上月 12.1%），化纤制造业
16.0%（上月 12.3%）；环比看，石油和天然气开采业上涨 6.0%（上月
10.4%）。二是煤炭价格维持高增，上行斜率 放缓。煤炭开采和洗选业价
格同比升至 36.6%（上月 35.3%），环比涨幅降至 2.7%（上月 9.5%）。供
给端约束叠加高油价替代效应，煤价维持高位，但经历前期快速上涨后边
际动能有所放缓。三是，有色链由前期持续走强转为降温。 有色金属矿采
选业同比降至 16.3%（上月 21.4%），有色冶炼同比降至 18.6%（上月
21.4%）。AI 产业链下游价格维持韧性，计算机通信和其他电子设备制造
业同比由 5.8%小幅升至 5.9%，电气机械和器材制造业维持 6.2%。 
生产资料与生活资料 PPI 分化延续，部分长期负增行业继续收窄。10
月，生产资料 PPI 同比上涨 7.4%，较 9 月提高 0.9 个百分点；生活资料
PPI 同比下降 0.1%，与 9 月持平，二者分化仍然明显。细分看，一方
面，部分此前长期低迷行业延续修复 。一是建筑材料链延续改善。非金制
品业价格同比降幅收窄至 1.6%（上月 2.5%），环比涨幅扩大至 0.8%（上
月 0.3%）；黑金冶炼和压延加工业同比上涨 0.4%（上月-0.2%），指向建
筑材料链延续了 9 月以来修复趋势，或与“反内卷”以来相关行业产能调
整、供求关系改善有关。二是多数长期负增的消费制造行业同比降幅继续
收窄。汽车制造业由-2.0%收窄至-1.8%，医药制造业由-3.3%收窄至-
3.2%，酒饮料和精制茶制造业由-5.3%收窄至-5.2%，农副食品加工业由-
0.9%收窄至-0.4%。另一方面，下游价格改善仍未形成全面扩散。 生活资
料中，衣着、一般日用品价格降幅小幅扩大，耐用消费品同比涨幅回落至
1.4%（上月 1.6%）；环比看，生活资料整体增速为-0.1%（上月 0.1%）。 
往后看，上下游价格分化存在进一步收敛的条件， 广谱性修复仍需内需接
力。一方面，中期选举后美国国内对进一步扩大中东冲突的约束有所增
强，前期推动油价快速上行的地缘风险溢价或有所回落，能源价格继续大
幅上冲的概率下降，输入性通胀对 PPI 上游价格的边际支撑可能趋弱。另
一方面，建筑材料及部分低位制造业价格已连续改善，叠加四季度政策项
目逐步形成实物工作量，黑色、建材等行业仍存在进一步修复的条件 。后
续价格结构性分化有望从两端同时收敛，一端是能源等上游行业价格上行
斜率放缓，另一端是建筑链及部分长期低迷行业逐步修复。总量上看，10
月核心 CPI 和生活资料 PPI 均未同步走强，指向当前下游修复尚未形成广
泛的内生价格动能。后续判断价格向广谱改善转变仍在于两条链条，一是
黑色、建材等价格改善能否在政策需求支撑下延续并向中游制造扩散； 二
是食品加工、汽车、医药等低位行业跌幅收窄能否进一步转化为生活资料
PPI、核心 CPI 和服务价格的持续改善。

风险提示：国际油价大幅波动超预期、国内需求修复不及预期。

投资建议的评级标准  评级 说明 
评级标准为报告发布日后的 6 个月内公司股价 （或行业
指数） 相对同期基准指数的相对市场表现。 其中A 股市
场以沪深 300 指数为基准； 新三板市场以三板成指 （针
对协议转让标的） 或三板做市指数 （针对做市转让标的）
为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准。 
股票评级 
买入 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 10%以上 
增持 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 5%~10%之间 
中性 相对同期基准指数涨幅在-5%~+5%之间 
减持 相对同期基准指数跌幅在 5%以上 
行业评级 
看好 相对表现优于市场 
中性 相对表现与市场持平 
看淡 相对表现弱于市场

Document 023: 158円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 ～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の“三つの分岐点”が方向性を決める～

Source PDF: all report pdf/158円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 ～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の“三つの分岐点”が方向性を決める～.pdf

158 円を挟んで膠着するドル円相場 
～中間選挙・イラン・金融政策の"三つの分岐点"が方向性を決める～ 
 経済調査部 シニア・フェロー  嶌峰 義清 
（℡：050-5474-7489）

（要旨） 
○ ドル円相場は、7 月末の日米協調介入、9 月上旬の急激な円高、9 月の日米同時利上げを経て、
10 月に入ってからは1 ドル＝158 円前後の狭いレンジでの推移が続いている。円先物市場に
おける非商業部門の建玉は、9 月前半に過去に例のない規模で円の売り越しから買い越しへ
と転じた。しかし、その後は買い越し幅が縮小し、売建玉が再び増え始めている。投機筋の
円買い意欲は弱まりつつあると言えよう。 
○ 期待インフレ率 （BEI） で実質化した日米の実質金利差は、政策金利・2 年債・10 年債のいず
れでも 2％ポイントを超える米国優位が続いている。名目金利差は縮小したものの、米国の
金利上昇は実質金利の上昇、日本のは期待インフレ率の上昇によるところが大きい。このた
め、実質ベースのドル優位は崩れていない。 
○ 当面の焦点は、①11 月 3 日の米中間選挙、②選挙後のイラン情勢、③日銀の 12 月利上げと
その後のペース、の三つである。民主党が上下両院を制し、イラン情勢が沈静化に向かい、
日銀が利上げ路線を明確にすれば、円高方向への転換が視野に入る。逆の組み合わせとなれ
ば、再び160 円台を試し、介入への警戒が強まる展開も否定できない。

1. 投機筋の円ポジションは"円売り一色"から変化し、158 円を挟んで膠着が続くドル円相場 
ドル円相場は、方向感を欠いた推移が続いている。10 月入り後の値動きは、高値が158.94 円
（10 月14 日） 、安値が156.99 円（10 月2 日）と、月間の値幅は2 円弱にとどまる。 
もっとも、ここに至るまでの道のりは平坦ではなかった。4 月末から5 月の大型連休中には、
160 円台後半まで進んだ円安に対して、政府・日銀が総額11.7 兆円の円買い介入を実施した。そ
れでも円安基調は止まらず、7 月 28 日には163.91 円（ECB参照値）と、約40 年ぶりの円安水準を
つけた。これに対し、7 月 31 日には日米当局が28 年ぶりとなる円買い協調介入に踏み切った。介
入額は 15 兆3993 億円に達し、年初からの累計は27 兆 1342 億円と暦年で過去最大を更新した。 
その後は円高へと自律的な反発も見られた。9 月2～3 日には、日銀の追加利上げ観測の強まり
や米雇用関連指標の下振れを受けてドル円は1 日で3 円以上下落、9 日には153 円台まで円高が進
んだ。この局面では介入実績は無く、ジャクソンホール会議でのウォーシュFRB議長の発言をきっ

### Page 2

2/6 
かけに積み上がったドル買い・円売りの持ち高が、損切りを迫られた結果と考えられる。 
ところが、その後は再び円安に押し戻された。FRBは 9 月 16 日に約3 年ぶりの利上げに踏み切
り、政策金利を3.75～4.00％とした。日銀も18 日に政策金利を1.25％へ引き上げたものの、日
米がそろって利上げしたことで金利差は大きく変わらず、ドル円は158 円台へと戻っている。 
為替市場における"投機的な動き"の指標とされるのが、円先物市場における非商業部門（主に
ヘッジファンドなどとされる）の建玉である。その推移を見ると、ここ数ヶ月で大きな変化が生
じていることがわかる（図表1）。 
6 月 2 日時点の円売建玉は24 万 4416 枚と、円キャリートレードがブームだった2007 年以来の
高水準に達していた。差引（ネット）でも12 万9567 枚の売り越しだった。その後も、9 月1 日時
点では 9 万2227 枚の売り越しと、円売り超の状態が続いていた。 
しかし、9 月前半の急激な円高を境に状況は一変し、9 月 15 日時点では差引が12 万 0359 枚の
買い越しへと転じ、わずか2 週間で円買い方向に21 万枚強も振れた。この振れ幅は、統計をさか
のぼれる1986 年以降で最大である。同時点の円買建玉（23 万 7951 枚）も過去最大を記録した。 
もっとも、その後は買い越し幅の縮小が続いている。10 月13 日時点では、買建玉19 万 1256 枚
に対し売建玉14 万 1049 枚で、差引の買い越しは5 万0207 枚とピークの半分以下に縮んだ。売建
玉は 9 月末の12 万枚台から14 万枚台へと再び増えており、円売りの持ち高が積み上がり始めて
いる兆しもうかがえる。投資主体別に見ると、レバレッジド・ファンドは10 月 13 日時点で3 万
3673 枚の円売り越しとなっており、ヘッジファンドの円買い意欲がすでに後退していることを示
している。 
足元の売建玉は、過去の介入局面のように当局が介入を急がなければならない水準にはない。
一方で、9 月に積み上がった円の買い持ちの解消そのものが、円安圧力として作用している面もあ
ると考えられる。

2.  実質金利差から見たドル円相場
為替相場の方向性を最終的に決めるのは、日米の金融政策と、それを反映した金利差である。
そこで、日米の実質金利差を、政策金利・2 年債・10 年債の3 つの年限で見てみる（図表2） 。こ
こでは、実質化に用いる物価として、市場が織り込む期待インフレ率であるBEI（ブレーク・イー
ブン・インフレ率、10 年債）を用いた。足元の消費者物価は、米国では原油高で押し上げられ、
日本では電気・ガス料金支援で押し下げられており、実勢を見誤りやすいためである。
名目で見ると、政策金利は米FF金利の誘導目標が3.75～4.00％、日本の無担保コール翌日物が
1.25％である。10 月 21 日時点の国債利回りは、米国が2 年債4.79％・10 年債 5.13％、日本が2
年債 1.886％・10 年債2.882％となっている。一方、10 年BEIは米国が2.35％（10 月20 日） 、日
本が 2.1～2.2％程度と、ほぼ同水準である。これを差し引いて実質化すると、筆者の試算ではお

おむね次のようになる。
✓ 政策金利：米国＋1.5～1.6％程度、日本▲0.9～1.0％程度（米国優位・差は約2.5％ポイント）
✓ 2 年債：米国＋2.4％程度、日本▲0.3％程度（米国優位・差は約2.7％ポイント）
✓ 10 年債：米国＋2.8％程度、日本＋0.7％程度（米国優位・差は約2.1％ポイント）
名目で見れば、日本の10 年債利回りが9 月下旬に3.115％と約 30 年ぶりの水準に達するなど、
日米金利差は縮小してきた。しかし、BEIで実質化すると景色は異なる。すべての年限で米国の実
質金利が2％ポイント以上も上回っており、ドル優位の構図はほとんど崩れていないのである。
その背景には、日米で金利上昇の"中身"が異なることがある。米国では、昨年末から長期金利
が約 1％ポイント上昇したが、BEIは 2.25％から2.35％へとほぼ横ばいにとどまった。名目金利の
注：BEIで実質化

### Page 4

4/6 
上昇は、ほぼそのまま実質金利の上昇となり、10 年物の実質利回りは9 月末に 2.93％と2008 年
以来の高水準に達した。これに対し日本では、BEIが過去最高圏の2％台まで上昇しており、名目
金利の上昇の相応の部分を期待インフレ率の上昇が占めている。実際、9 月の日銀の利上げ後も
BEIはむしろ上昇した。日本の金利上昇は"インフレ期待による金利上昇"の色彩が濃く、実質金利
の上昇は限られていると言えよう。 
日本の政策金利は、実質では依然として▲1％近いマイナス圏にあり、金融環境はなお緩和的
だ。日銀の利上げや介入にもかかわらず円安の流れが変わらなかったのは、こうした実質金利差
の構図で説明できる面が大きい。逆に言えば、日本の期待インフレ率が2％近辺で落ち着いたまま
利上げが進むか、米国の実質金利が低下に転じない限り、本格的な円高への転換は難しいと考え
られる。 
なお、日本の物価連動国債は発行残高が約10 兆円と小さく、BEIには流動性の影響も含まれ
る。政策金利や2 年債を10 年BEIで実質化するのも簡便的な手法である点には留意が必要だ。

3.  中間選挙の結果次第で変わるドルの行方 
11 月3 日には米国の中間選挙が行われる。1934 年以降の23 回の中間選挙のうち、現職大統領
の政党が上下両院とも議席を増やしたのはわずか2 回しかない。今回も、ロイター通信の世論調
査ではトランプ大統領の支持率が過去最低の31％まで落ち込み、投票先では民主党47％、共和党
35％と 12 ポイントの差がついている。選挙予測サイトのディシジョン・デスクHQは、民主党が下
院を制する確率を78％、上院を制する確率を56％と見ている。 
結果ごとに、ドル円相場への影響を整理すると次のようになる。 
① 民主党が上下両院を制する場合：予算や減税、関税政策に対する議会のチェックが強まり、
財政拡張への期待が後退することで、米長期金利の低下とドル安につながりやすい。FRBの人事に
対する議会の影響力が強まり、金融政策の独立性への懸念が和らぐことも、長期金利の押し下げ
要因となろう。一方で、予算案を巡る対立が先鋭化すれば、政府機関の閉鎖などを通じたリスク
回避の円買いが生じる可能性もある。いずれにしても、ドル円には下押し圧力がかかる公算が大
きい。 
② 下院は民主党、上院は共和党の"ねじれ"となる場合：現在の政策の大枠が維持される一方
で、新たな大型財政出動は難しくなる。相場の反応は限定的で、関心は再び日米の金融政策へと
移ることになろう。 
③ 共和党が両院の過半数を維持する場合：減税や規制緩和など拡張的な政策への期待から、米
長期金利の上昇とドル高が進みやすい。ドル円が再び160 円台を試し、介入への警戒が一気に高
まる展開も想定される。 
世論調査どおりであれば①ないし②の公算が大きく、ドル円にはどちらかと言えば円高寄りの
材料となろう。ただし、重要なのは選挙後の財政・通商政策や金利見通しの変化であり、選挙直
後の値動きだけで方向性を判断するのは早計だろう。

### Page 5

5/6 
4.  イラン情勢：選挙後の"重し"が外れる 
もう一つの大きな不透明要因がイラン情勢である。2 月 28 日に始まった米国・イスラエルとイ
ランの戦闘は、ホルムズ海峡の事実上の封鎖と、米国によるイラン港湾の海上封鎖を伴ったまま
長期化している。北海ブレント原油先物は20 日に一時1 バレル＝111.99 ドルと約5 ヶ月ぶりの高
値をつけた。 
原油高は、いくつかの経路でドル高円安に作用している。米国では、ガソリン価格の前年比
34％の上昇などを背景に、9 月の消費者物価が同＋3.7％へと加速し、FRBの追加利上げ観測を支え
ている。日本にとっては、エネルギー輸入額の増加を通じて貿易収支が悪化し、実需の円売りが
増える。加えて、地政学リスクの高まりは"有事のドル買い"も誘いやすい。 
注目されるのは、トランプ大統領が「中間選挙前にはイランを攻撃しない」と明言している点
だ。言い換えれば、選挙後はこの制約が外れることになる。選挙後に軍事行動が再開されれば、
原油価格の一段高と米利上げ観測の強まりを通じて、ドル高円安が進むリスクがある。逆に、仲
介国を通じた交渉がまとまり、海峡の再開に向かえば、原油価格の下落とともに米長期金利が低
下し、ドル安円高へと振れやすい。実際、20～21 日には原油安を受けて米長期金利が低下し、ド
ルが売られる場面が見られた。

5.  日米の金融政策：12 月の"同時利上げ"の先が焦点 
FRBは9 月に約3 年ぶりの利上げに踏み切り、年内にもう1 回の利上げを示唆した。10 月14 日
に9 月の消費者物価が前年比3.7％と予想を上回ると、27～28 日のFOMCでの利上げ確率は一時5
割前後まで上昇した。しかし、17 日にウォーシュ議長が10 月会合の判断を予断しない姿勢を示し
たことで、利上げ確率は1 割台半ばまで低下している。市場では「10 月は見送り、12 月に利上
げ」との見方が大勢となった。一部には、12 月が今回の利上げ局面で最後になるとの予想も出て
いる。 
一方、日銀の植田総裁は、9 月の利上げ後に「政策の局面は変化した」と述べ、その後も基調的
な物価上昇率が2％に「かなり近づいている」との認識を示した。そのうえで、 「これ以上上がる
ことは容認できない」とも踏み込んでいる。29～30 日の決定会合では政策金利が据え置かれる公
算が大きいが、基調物価が2％に到達したと判断されると報じられている。 
日米がともに12 月に利上げすれば、名目の政策金利差は変わらない。為替にとって重要なの
は、その先の方向性の違いである。FF金利が明確に中立水準を超えるFRBの利上げが最終局面に近
づく一方、日銀は中立金利を目指して利上げを続ける。この構図が鮮明になれば、日米の実質金
利差は徐々に縮小に向かおう。逆に、原油高を受けて米国の物価上昇が加速し、FRBが一段の利上
げを迫られる展開となれば、実質金利差の縮小は見込めず、ドル円相場の膠着状態を演出しよ
う。

6.  今後の見通し：年末に向けて緩やかな円高を想定、ただし分岐は大きい 
以上を踏まえると、当面のドル円相場は、日米の金融政策会合と中間選挙という大きなイベン

### Page 6

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
6/6 
トを控え、当面は現状の狭いレンジでの推移が続くと考えられる。10 月27～28 日のFOMCは据え置
きが市場の大勢だ。FOMCで利上げが見送られ、その後の日銀会合で基調物価2％到達が確認されて
12 月利上げへの地ならしが進めば、円高方向に振れやすい組み合わせとなる。しかし、市場では
FRBも年内利上げを実施するとの見方が強く、ドル円相場を動かす材料とはなりにくい。逆に、
FRBが10 月のFOMCで利上げを決定した場合、ドル円相場は159 円を超える円安進展の可能性が高
まる。 
年末にかけては、①中間選挙で民主党が議会の主導権を握り、②イラン情勢が交渉による解決
に向かい原油価格が落ち着き、③日銀が12 月に利上げを実施して来年以降も利上げ継続の姿勢を
示す、という三つの条件がそろえば、日米の実質金利差は縮小に向かう。その場合は、150 円台半
ばから前半への円高が進む可能性がある。投機筋の売建玉が再び積み上がっている局面であれ
ば、その巻き戻しが円高の勢いを増幅することも考えられる。 
一方で、①共和党の善戦、②選挙後のイランへの軍事行動の再開、③FRBの利上げ継続＋日銀の
利上げ慎重姿勢打ち出し、といった組み合わせとなれば、ドル円は再び160 円台を試すことにな
ろう。その場合、政府・日銀は過去の介入水準である160 円台前半から164 円近辺を意識しつ
つ、円安のスピードを抑えるための介入に踏み切る公算が大きい。ただし、これまでの経験が示
すように、ファンダメンタルズが変わらない中での介入は"時間を買う"政策にすぎず、効果が一
時的にとどまる可能性には注意が必要だ。 
筆者は、①米中間選挙は最低でも上下院のねじれ、場合によっては上院においても民主党の過
半数獲得、②選挙後に米国がイランへの軍事行動を再開、同時に仲介国を介した交渉を再開する
ものの、膠着状態が続く、③日米とも利上げ継続、をメインシナリオとしている。 
さらにリスク要因として、①米中間選挙の結果がなかなか確定せず、議会勢力の確定に時間が
かかる、②イラン情勢はフーシ派から攻撃を受けているサウジアラビアや、総選挙を終えたイス
ラエルによる本格的な攻撃参戦、の可能性を挙げる。 
このように不透明要因が多いことも足元のドル円相場の膠着の要因になっていると考えられる
が、短期的な値動きに一喜一憂するよりも、実質金利差という基本的な決定要因がどちらに動い
ているかを冷静に見極めることが重要であり、日米中央銀行の金融政策姿勢が最も注目される。

2  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/
上週重要市場資訊
 美國期中選舉由民主黨贏得參議院及眾議院多數席次，川普剩餘兩年任內將面
臨分立政府的局面，市場擔憂民主黨未來傾向加強AI監管，恐削弱美國經濟成
長動能。
 歐元區 10月 CPI年增率自前月的 3.8%上升至 4%，創下三年來的新高，通膨面
臨上行風險。歐洲央行管委暨斯洛伐克央行總裁卡日米爾(Peter Kazimir)表示，
歐洲央行將持續升息，以防止高通膨根深蒂固；德國央行總裁納格爾 (Joachim
Nagel)亦表示通膨前景尚未改善，歐洲央行應於 12 月升息。多位歐洲央行官員
發表偏鷹言論，市場預期歐洲央行未來一年內將再升息二至三次，升息預期升
溫，預計將支撐歐元走勢。
 美國 10 月 ISM 製造業 PMI 為 55.5，高於市場預期，製造業擴張速度加快；另
10 月 ISM非製造業 PMI為 54.5，持穩於景氣榮枯線之上，顯示美國整體基本面
佳，預計將為美元提供支撐。

 美國 10 月非農就業人數增加 16.5 萬人，優於市場預期的 6.5 萬人，另 10 月失
業率亦由前月的 4.2%降至 4.1%，顯示勞動市場仍具韌性，在美國經濟穩健及通
膨維持高位的情況下，強化聯準會 12月升息預期。
 澳洲央行於 11/3 將現金基準利率調升一碼至 4.85%，係今年以來第五次升息並
創下 2008 年 11 月以來新高水準。然而，此次九名委員中有三人主張維持利率
不變，投票結果出現分歧，加上貨幣政策委員會聲明中，刪除有關可能進一步升
息的明確表述，令市場預期澳洲央行將於 12月暫停升息。

3  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
 美元兌新台幣匯率走勢  
Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
    上週台幣匯價高點為 32.500，低點在 33.040，以 32.768作收，較前一週收盤價
貶值 2 角 9 分。 
    上週台幣走貶，週初，台股開低，市場對AI投資支出及科技股估值的疑慮，致
使外資連續多日賣超台股並匯出，台幣連貶數日，加以週間美國期中選舉結果出爐，
民主黨勝選，市場擔憂 AI產業監管趨嚴，台股重挫，外資上週累計賣超台股 5,244
億，外資強勢匯出之下，儘管逢高有出口商拋匯抑制台幣貶值速度，台幣仍貶破33
整數大關，一度觸及年內低點 33.040，惟週五午盤過後，外資及壽險轉向匯入，加
以出口商拋匯需求，台幣跌幅收斂，終場在央行調節之下收在 32.768。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。就國際市場而言，美國非農就業數據意外高於預期、
通膨壓力仍在，強化市場對於聯準會12月升息預期，高利率將有助國際美元走升；
就台灣市場來看，市場對AI產業資本支出、融資成本及信用風險產生疑慮，加以民
主黨勝選，引發市場對AI監管政策收緊與資料中心建設放緩的擔憂，外資持續賣超
台股，令台幣持續面臨貶值的壓力，惟出口商拋匯需求待消化，將抑制台幣貶值速
度，綜合以上，預估本週台幣走勢偏弱。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌台幣：32.600-33.200

上週人民幣先升後貶。週初，國際美元偏弱震盪，人民幣受益走升，境內人民
幣相隔四年多首次升破 6.65整數大關 ； 惟週二中國央行宣布調升外匯存款準備金率 ，

影響，人民幣走貶，最低一度貶至 6.6855，不過美國期中選舉結果出爐，市場不確
定性下降，國際油價、美債殖利率及美元指數皆下跌，緩解人民幣的賣壓，惟中國
國有大行逢低持續買進美元，人民幣反彈力道有限，終場境外人民幣收在 6.6736，
週線貶值 212 點，境內人民幣則收在 6.6763，週線貶值 203 點。

＜未來分析＞ 
     預估本週人民幣區間震盪。就國際市場來看，美國非農就業人數新增 16.5 萬
人，就業率下降至 4.1%，勞動力市場意外強勁，強化市場對於聯準會 12 月再次升
息的預期，利差因素支撐美元指數反彈，將抑制人民幣走升；就中國國內市場來看，
人行最新政策顯示其抑制人民幣單邊升值的意圖，惟第四季為結匯旺季，出口商實
質拋匯需求部分緩解人民幣貶值壓力；最後就技術面來看，USD/CNH 日線圖中，
USD/CNH重返五日均線之上，顯示人民幣強勢升值的趨勢暫歇，綜合以上，預估本
週人民幣區間震盪。

＜預估本週＞ 美元兌人民幣：6.6600-6.6900

5  兆豐國際商業銀行 http://www.megabank.com.tw/ 
美元兌日圓匯率走勢  
         Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
上週日圓走弱。週初，市場等待美國期中選舉結果，交投偏謹慎觀望，USD/JPY
在 158 附近波動；週間，美國期中選舉由民主黨取得參眾議院多數席次，政治僵局
擔憂令美元走弱，USD/JPY最低來到 157.39；臨近週末，美國公布就業數據優於市
場預期，USD/JPY止跌回升，終場收在 158.45，週線上漲 100 點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 USD/JPY區間震盪，主要原因如下：一、日本央行於季度展望報告中
表示，日本近期實際利率上升，基礎通膨率已加速升至2%附近，加上日本央行近期
亦強調政策已進入避免通膨超標為重點的新階段，進一步強化了其鷹派立場，市場
預期日本最快將於 12月再次升息，預計將支撐日圓走升。二、美國10月 ISM製造
業 PMI 為 55.5，優於市場預期，顯示製造業擴張速度加快；另外，美國公布 10 月
非農就業人數增加 16.5 萬人，優於市場預期的 6.5 萬人，美國經濟強勁、勞動市場
仍具韌性，強化聯準會12月升息預期，美元持穩，預計將有利USD/JPY走勢。三、
日本財務大臣片山皋月重申美日聯合匯率聲明仍具效力，將於必要時採取外匯干預
行動，以維護外匯市場有序運作，將限縮USD/JPY上行空間。四、就技術面而言，
在日線圖中，USD/JPY短中天期均線趨平，近期大致在157 至 158.50間盤整，匯價
暫無明確方向性。綜合以上，預估 USD/JPY本週呈現區間震盪的可能性較高。

美元兌歐元匯率走勢  
             Nov/09/2026

＜上週走勢＞ 
  上週 EUR/USD 先上後下。週初，在美伊傳出有望和談消息後，歐元自低點小
幅走升至 1.1237；週間，美國民主黨於期中選舉大獲全勝，川普選舉失利，美元偏
弱震盪；臨近週末，美國公布非農就業人數高於市場預期，歐元自高點回落，終場
收在 1.1143，週線下跌 12點。 
＜未來分析＞ 
  預估本週 EUR/USD震盪偏弱，主要原因如下：一、歐元區10月 CPI年增率自
前月的 3.8%上升至 4%，創下三年來的新高，通膨面臨上行風險。此外，歐洲央行
管委暨斯洛伐克央行總裁卡日米爾(Peter Kazimir)表示，歐洲央行將持續升息，以防
止高通膨根深蒂固，並表示目前主要考量已非是否需採取進一步行動，而是須衡量
應升息幾次方能將通膨拉回 2%目標水準，市場預期歐洲央行未來一年內將再升息
二至三次，升息預期升溫，預計將支撐歐元走勢。二、美國公布10月非農就業人數
增加 16.5 萬人，優於市場預期的 6.5 萬人，另 10 月失業率亦由前月的 4.2%降至
4.1%，顯示勞動市場仍具韌性，強化了聯準會12月升息預期，美元再度走升至102，
預計將限縮歐元走升空間。三、技術面而言，在日線圖中，EUR/USD各天期均線向
下發散，近期匯價多次向上測試1.1270均拉回留下長上影線，顯示上方仍有賣壓待
消化。綜合以上，預估 EUR/USD本週呈現震盪偏弱的可能性較高。

                           新台幣利率走勢分析

115.11.02-115.11.06 
銀行隔夜拆款利率 0.805% ~ 0.825%  
30天期短票利率   1.67% ~ 1.69%  
公債－10 年期-A15109R 1.885% 
－ 5 年期- A15108 1.725%

一、 台幣貨幣市場部分，受台股重挫影響，令週五資金需求上揚，但提存期初市場
資金充裕，短率持穩於近期區間。 
週二央行標售 364天期定存單 1100億元，加權平均得標利率為1.577%，低於
市場預期區間 1.59%-1.61%，較上次標售結果下跌 2.5個基本點。 
週五隔夜拆款利率區間在 0.805%~0.825%， 30 天期短票主要成交在
1.67%~1.69%，利率較上週上揚 1 個基本點。 
二、 本週市場交投維持冷清，10 年期指標券 A15109R及 5年期指標券 A15108均
未成交，週五等殖系統參考報價中價分別為 1.885%、1.725%。 
股市承壓帶動避險需求，美債利率下滑，令先前台債偏空氛圍緩解，週三標售
30年期公債得標利率 2.267%， 低於市場預期區間2.27%~2.30%， 投標倍數1.85
倍，較上次 1.55 倍提升，不過考量台灣年底仍有升息可能，將抑制買盤向下
追價意願，預估台債利率區間盤整，預估 5 年券區間為 1.70%~1.75%，10 年
券區間為 1.85%~1.90%。

E Ink Holdings Inc.  | Asia Pacific
3Q26 Missed on Margins
No strong near-term catalysts despite trough valuation.
3Q26  results:
• Off revenue of NT$9,936mn (-3% QoQ/-5% YoY), GM declined 6.5ppt QoQ
to 52.2%, significantly below MSe at 57.6%/consensus at 58.0%. We think
this is probably due to less-favorable revenue mix.
• Operating profit of NT$2,643mn (OPM 26.6%) was 21% lower than MSe at
NT$3,330mn (OPM 33.4%)/19% off consensus at NT$3,281mn (OPM 33.6%).

• Net profit  at NT$2,652mn (EPS of NT$2.31) was 24% below MSe at NT
$3,491mn (EPS NT$3.03)/20% off consensus at NT$3,307mn (EPS NT$2.79).
October revenue was NT$3,747mn (+4% MoM/+74% YoY):
• 33% of both our 4Q26 revenue estimate at NT$11,459mn (+15% QoQ/+63%
YoY) and  consensus of NT$11,263mn (+13% QoQ/+61% YoY).
Other announcements (see more in Other announcements ):
•   E Ink announced spending of NT$2.78bn acquiring the land and factories in
Guanyin (Taiwan) from its related party Chunghwa Pulp  for its large-size

signage capacity.
• It  partners with Taiflex  for the latter to do CCL pilot production at E Ink's
facility, with potentially room for collaboration on mass production in the
future.
• It announced it would invest NT$187mn in the rights issuance from its
related party SinoPac FHC.
Our view:
• 4Q26 revenue will need to grow by 10% QoQ to put overall 2026 revenue at
10% YoY (low end of its guidance at 10-15% YoY), which implies Nov-26 and
Dec-26 could be down low single digits % MoM each. While it still looks

within reach, we suspect this is at least partially riding the tailwinds of more
module shipments, which might continue to suppress its gross margin.
• Although the  valuation is at a trough level of 11x 2027e P/E, we see no
strong catalysts in the near term except for the potential fund flows amid
the subdued AI appetite. That said, we remain a believer of longer-term e-
paper demand across various applications.
• 3Q26 earnings call is scheduled at 2:30pm HKT on November 12.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
E Ink Holdings Inc. (8069.TWO)
Base case, 12x 2027e P/E. We believe this valuation methodology best captures E Ink's earn-
ings growth momentum. We view our target multiple of 12x as fair, considering our estimated
17% earnings CAGR for 2025-28. The implied PEG of 0.7x is largely in line with its typical level
in 2022-25, when the stock was trading at an average P/E of 22x one-year forward consensus
EPS with a 37% earnings CAGR.

Risks to Upside
n Firmer-than-expected ESL orders
n Stronger demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Better macro conditions
n Milder competition
n Brisker inventory digestion
Risks to Downside
n Softer-than-expected ESL orders
n Slower demand for e-readers and e-notes
n Macro headwinds
n Intensified competition
n More protracted inventory digestion

12-18 months.

### Page 1

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
3Q26 Beat on Margins Reinforces AI Thermal Ramp; TP Maintained at 
NT$4,350
CITI'S TAKE 
AVC reported net profit of NT$11.6bn, topping Coto/cons estimates by 
2%/2% respectively, driven by margins beat partly offset by a non-ops FX 
loss. GPM improvement was above expectations on a richer liquid cooling 
mix, yield gains and disciplined raw material pass-through. Mgmt. guided 
positively for 4Q26 and 2027, led by AI ASIC ramp-up and Vera Rubin ramp 
up. We model 16% QoQ revenue growth for 4Q26E. GPM guidance also 
points to further sequential improvement on a higher liquid cooling mix and 
scale effects. Mgmt. reiterated 2026E/27E capex of NT$15bn/15-20bn, 
alongside 50%+ cold plate capacity expansion, underscoring capacity 
conviction. We fine-tune our earnings forecasts to reflect updated guidance 
and maintain TP at NT$4,350 (25x 2027E EPS). Reiterate Buy.
3Q26 results beat on margins — AVC’s reported 3Q26 net profit of NT$11.6bn 
(+20% QoQ/+52% YoY) beat Citi/cons forecast by 2%/2%, driven by better-than-
expected margins, partly offset by a non-ops loss. 3Q26 GPM came in at 33.9% 
(+1.3ppts QoQ/+7.7 ppts YoY), above both Citi/cons estimates, mainly due to 
continued mix improvement, a higher liquid cooling contribution, yield gains, and 
disciplined pass-through of raw material price fluctuations to customers. Non-ops 
included a NT$304mn FX loss, which partially offset the strong core business.
ASIC ramp and capacity guidance underpin long-term outlook — Mgmt. guided 
positively for 4Q26 and 2027, led by AI ASIC project ramp-up and early Vera Rubin 
generation contribution. We now model 16% QoQ revenue growth for 4Q26E. GPM 
guidance also points to further sequential improvement vs. 3Q26, driven by a higher 
liquid cooling mix and scale effects. Mgmt. expects server compute tray liquid 
cooling penetration to reach 100% by end-2027. Mgmt. reiterated 2026E/26E capex 
guidance of NT$15bn/15-20bn, alongside 50%+ cold plate capacity expansion, 
reinforcing confidence in capacity build-out.
Implications — We fine-tune our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts to reflect updated 
guidance and outlook. We maintain our TP at NT$4,350, still based on 25x 2027E EPS. 
We believe AVC’s solid supply position across key customers’ AI GPU and ASIC projects, 
combined with a sustained growth trajectory, continues to support its valuation. With 
visibility extending into 2027, we see limited near-term read-through risk from the 
ongoing platform transition. We reiterate our Buy rating on AVC.

Bull/Bear: Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)

### Page 6

Asia Vital Components (3017.TW)
10 November 2026 Citi Research

Asia Vital Components
Company description
Asia Vital Components (AVC), founded in 1991, is a global leader in thermal
solutions, originally focusing on PCs but now specializing in servers and
networking. With Furukawa Electric (Japan) as its largest shareholder, AVC
provides a comprehensive range of cooling components, including vapor
chambers (VCs), fans, cold plates, CDUs, and manifolds, as well as chassis

and assembly services for PCs and servers, enabling holistic thermal solution
designs and one-stop shop services. AVC's diverse product portfolio supports
various sectors, including consumer IT, cloud infrastructure, transportation,
energy and networking.
Investment strategy
We rate AVC a Buy. AVC is well-positioned for the liquid-cooling transition,
with leadership in cold plates and broad participation across Nvidia and CSP
projects. Its high-volume machining capability, manufacturing uniformity,

and Vietnam-based capacity provide it with a defensible position as
Blackwell and future deployments expand. The company captures a wider
share of the thermal BOM, across coldplates, manifolds, QDs, and CDU
opportunities, providing a multi-layered earnings engine.
Valuation
We value AVC using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum
and the expansion of its addressable market. Our TP of NT$4,350 is derived
from applying a 25x PE multiple on 2027E EPS, which we believe is justified

given the company’s entrenched positioned in the server thermal segment,
rising contribution from liquid cooling, and the increasing value of thermal
content per server as power density continues to climb. With these structural
drivers still in place, we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its
historical averages.
Risks
This stock is High Risk based upon our quantitative model but assigning a
High Risk rating is not supported by other qualitative factors such as cold-

plate leadership and deep CSP penetration, and so a High Risk rating has not
been applied. Key downside risks to our investment thesis and target price
include: 1) slower adoption pace for liquid cooling adoption; 2) heightened
price competition; 3) lower-than-expected share in liquid cooling
components; and 4) emerging alternative cooling technologies.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

• Anticipating another strong year into 2027 — MPI has maintained its 
current guidance for capacity plan into next year: year end-2026 capacity 
targets of VPC 2m and MEMS 3.5m, and 2027 MEMS expansion is expected 
to be at least another 2.5m. However, we see potential upside thanks to 
robust customer demand and market share gain. CPO insertion 2 is under 
final evaluation and insertion 3 already starts engineering  shipments  to 
more than 5 international customers across foundry, OSAT and fabless 
segments. The company sees strong growth momentum of probe card into 
next year and CPO equipment contribution at single digit in 2027 with more 
upside into 2028.
• We stay constructive on MPI despite recent market concerns regarding AI 
demand slowing down. The key drivers are continuing to ramp-up at 
existing AI ASIC customers, incremental contributions from new AI/non-AI 
ASIC and CPU customers, further MEMS capacity expansion, and potential 
upside from CPO and microbump probe-card technology.

2
MPI
Valuation
Our target price for MPI is set at NT$7,200, based on a target PE multiple of 52x applied to our 2027-28E EPS average. We
believe our PE target, at the high end of MPI's 3-year forward PE range, is justified given MPI’s earnings power remains
structurally higher than that in the previous cycles, broadening demand across AI ASIC, additional CPU opportunities and non-
AI ASIC wins. We expect MPI to see accelerating earnings growth ahead supported by sustainable sales growth and an

improving mix. At our target price, the shares would trade at 2027E/28E P/B of 23x/15x.
Risks
Our quant risk rating system rates the MPI stock as High Risk for its share-price volatility. Key downside risks that could
prevent MPI shares from reaching our target price include 1) slowdown in AI ASIC demand; 2) slower-than-expected adoption
of MEMS probe cards; 3) intensifying competition from global and regional peers; 4) slow development of the CPO equipment

business.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do

### Page 1

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
3Q26 Beat on Margins from ASP Pass-Through and Richer Mix
CITI'S TAKE 
MSI reported 3Q26 net profit of NT$4.0bn (+34% QoQ/+138% YoY), 
beating Citi/cons by 27%/45% on stronger-than-expected margins and 
non-op gains. GPM of 17.0% (+1.1ppts QoQ/+5.8 ppts YoY) topped Citi/cons 
at 15.8%/14.5%, as ASP hikes passed through rising component costs while 
moving-avg inventory costs lagged, alongside a richer high-end product 
mix. For 4Q26E, we expect further ASP increases and a rising server 
contribution to largely offset seasonal shipment declines. Into 2027, we 
expect PC demand to stay weak and pass-through room to narrow as 
memory price increases moderately. We raise 2026E-28E earnings and our 
TP to NT$145 (10x 2027E EPS). We stay Neutral as the share price already 
reflects the pass-through upside.
3Q26: Margin-led beat on cost pass-through and better mix — MSI reported 
3Q26 net profit of NT$4.0bn (+34% QoQ/+138% YoY), beating Citi/cons forecasts 
by 27%/45%, mainly on stronger-than-expected margins and non-op gains. GPM 
of 17.0% (+1.1ppts QoQ/+5.8ppts YoY) came in above Citi/cons at 15.8%/14.5%. 
Mgmt. attributed the upside to 1) ASP increase, as MSI raised prices in line with 
suppliers’ cost increases while its moving-average inventory costs rose less; and 2) 
a higher mix of high-end products. Non-op gains, including FX gains of over 
NT$100mn, further boosted the bottom line.
Price hikes to continue; 2027 PC market to keep shrinking — We reiterate our view 
that 4Q26 shipments will decline seasonally from the 3Q26 peak. However, mgmt. 
expects rising raw material costs and continued pass-through to keep lifting ASPs, 
offsetting lower volumes and making the seasonal decline milder than in prior years. 
The server business should also contribute more, potentially reaching a high-single-
digit revenue share. Into 2027, we expect weak PC demand amid elevated costs, 
with MSI’s MB and PC shipments declining by over 10%. Pass-through room should 
narrow as memory price increases moderately, though margins should stay elevated 
for a while, as mgmt. expects memory supply to stay tight in the coming quarters.
Implications — We raise our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts by 6-27%, reflecting 
sustained strong component cost pass-through and higher margin assumptions. 
We adjust TP to NT$145 (from NT$140), implying 10x 2027E EPS (from 12x). We 
believe the share price already reflects MSI’s opportunity to pass component costs 
through to end products, and we expect limited upside going forward, given that the 
PC demand recovery remains highly uncertain. Maintain Neutral.

### Page 6

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
13 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Micro-Star
Company description
Established in 1986 and listed in 1998, Taiwan-based MSI is a leading
OBM/ODM for gaming PCs, motherboards, graphic cards, industrial PCs, and
servers. MSI started business as a motherboard manufacturer but
transformed itself to become a leading gaming PC play in 2012 with a
comprehensive product portfolio across the gaming system and components.
MSI enjoys about 16% global share in the gaming NB industry, and is ranked

No.3 in graphic card shipments and No.3 in motherboard shipments globally.
Investment strategy
We rate MSI shares at Neutral as we anticipate earnings upward pressure
from the delince in PC and MB/VGA shipments in the near-term. While the
soaring component costs could be partially offset by price hike, we expect
muted demand and AI resource crowding to wiegh on margins upside. Further
upside maintains limited, though, until we see a meaningful recovery in core

gaming demand or a more substantial contribution from the server business.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$145 is based on 10x 2027E EPS, below the stock's five-
year average forward 12-month PE. This valuation reflects the earnings
downside risks from muted PC demand and margin pressure from soaring
component costs, while accounting for the time required for the server
business to achieve meaningful scale.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause MSI's shares to trade above our target price

include: 1) a sharper-than-expected recovery in high-end gaming demand;
and 2) faster-than-anticipated revenue contribution from the AI server
segment. Key downside risks include: 1) a deeper-than-expected contraction
in global PC shipments; 2) prolonged supply shortages for gaming GPUs due
to Ai resource crowding; and 3) inability to pass through surging memory and
CPU costs to price-sensitive consumers.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

주가 추이

3Q26 매출액과 영 업 이 익 은 모두 당사 추정치를 크게 하회했습니다. 탁월
한 차량 판매 증가세에도 불구하고 부정적 믹스와 환율/원자재가 상승에 따
른 수익성 악화가 아 쉬 웠 습 니 다. 매크로 영향 고려한 추 정 치 하향 및 로봇 
지분가치 할인으로 목표주가 21만원으로 조정합니다. 
큰 폭의 판매 성장에도 수익성 크게 악화 
동사 3Q26 매출액은 31.6조원(+10.1% 이하 YoY), 영업이익 1.8조원
(+24.3%)을 기록하며 추정치를 크게 하회했다. 관세 영향이 본격화되었
던 전년 동기 대비 관세율 차이에 따른 비용 감소(5.3천억원)와 판매보증 
충당부채 전입액 감소(6.7천억원) 등 낮은 기저로 이익이 개선되었으나, 
2023년 생산 정상화 이후 가장 큰 폭의 판매 성장(84.7만대, +7.9%)에도 
불구하고 인센티브와 재료비 상승, 중동 물량 감소와 경제형 EV 판매 확
대에 따른 부정적 믹스로 OPM 5.8%에 그친 실적을 기록했다.  
예상보다 큰 부정적 PT/지역 믹스 영향 
기대를 상회하는 판매량 증가에 따라 물량 효과 통한 수익성 개선을 기
대했으나, 예상 대비 큰 3Q 부정적 믹스 영향에 따라 4Q와 ’27년 실적 
개선 기대 또한 낮출 수밖에 없는 상황이다. 아중동 판매 부진과 유럽 모
델 수익성 저하 영향이 판매 물량 증가 효과보다 크게 작용하고 있다. 인
도, 중남미 대비 고마진 차종 믹스가 좋은 중동의 지정학적 영향이 지속
되고 있고, 유럽은 EV2 등 신차 중심 판매 확대에도 중국 로컬업체들의 
유럽 공세 격화로 시장 점유율을 사수하기 위한 모델 저수익화가 예상 
대비 장기화될 것으로 판단한다. 그러나 고정비 만회를 위해 인센티브 
증가와 가격 효과 축소 영향 속에서도 판매 확대는 불가피한 상황이다. 
부정적 상황 장기화 가능성, 목표주가 210,000원으로 하향 
결국, 수익성 개선을 위해서는 고마진 제품 판매 확대 또는 판매가 견조
하나 저마진 차종 원가를 절감하는 방법밖에 없다. 북미 텔루라이드 
HEV 판매와 스포티지HEV HMGMA 증산이 고마진 물량 증가에 지속
기여할 것이다. 그러나 단기 중동 시장 회복과 차종별 원가절감 효과 확
인이 어렵다는 점에서 고마진 제품 판매 확대 효과는 제한적 전망이다. 
이에, 투자의견 Buy 유지하되, 목표주가 21만원으로 조정한다. 실적 추
정치 조정하여 영업가치를 종전 대비 15.7% 하향하고, 성장주 밸류 디스
카운트에 따른 로봇 지분가치 30% 할인하였다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 11 월 2 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 7

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

7 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

3Q26 Review: “조삼모사” Again  
3Q26P 연결 실적은 매출액 6조 943억원(YoY -6.0%), 영업이익 4,520억원(YoY -46.2%, OPM 
7.4%)을 기록하며 컨센서스 대비 연결 매출액(7.5조원)과 영업이익(1.16조원) 모두 대폭 하회했다. 주
요 자회사가 양호한 실적을 기록했음에도 불구하고, 지상방산 실적이 매우 부진했다. 지상방산은 매출
액 1조 2,455억원(YoY -41.0%), 영업이익 217억원(YoY -96.2%, OPM 1.7%)을 기록했는데, 1)3Q 
생산 물량이 4Q 매출 인식되는 흐름 속, 고정비 부담이 컸었고, 2)낮은 수출 비중(3Q 48%, 2Q 58%)
과 국내외 사업의 부진한 믹스(국내 BEP 수준, 해외 OPM 11% 추정), 3)비우호적인 원/달러 환율, 4)
대전 사업장 사고 복구 및 기타 일회성 비용(300억원+160억원, 총 460억원) 등이 반영되었다.   
대전 사업장 정상화 시점과 사업 차질 영향은? 
대전 사업장은 내년 상반기 중 정상화될 예정이며, 보은과 여수 공장에서 생산 보완을 위한 조치가 진
행 중인 것으로 소통했다. 또한 대전 사업장은 국내 물량 위주이며, 수출 납기(천무, 천궁-II 추진체 
등)에는 지장이 없을 것이라고 언급했지만, 기타 사업장 램프업을 위한 인건비 증가 등, 수익성 악화는 
불가피할 것으로 예상된다.  
15%대의 매출 성장 가이던스는 달성이 가능할까? 
보수적으로 접근하는 것이 합리적이라는 판단이다. 당사는 ‘26E 지상방산 매출액을 일부 ‘27~28E로 
이연하면서 -6.1% 하향 조정했다. 사측은 폴란드 EC1 천무 인도 가이던스를 기존 40문에서 60문으
로 상향 조정하는 등 납품 속도가 예정대비 빨라진 것을 언급했다. 다만, 천무 EC1에서 미사일 물량 
비중이 높은 것으로 파악되기 때문에 생산 지연 가능성과 수출입 데이터 상 12월 출하 물량의 매출 인
식 이월 가능성을 반영했다. ‘27E는 폴란드 후속 사업과 루마니아 K9, 호주 레드백 등이 양산 페이즈
에 진입하며 성장세가 지속될 전망이다.    
투자의견 Buy, 목표주가 1,480,000원 유지 
실적 쇼크와는 별개로, 미국 K9MH 시제, 스페인 K9, 크로아티아 천무 등 수주는 서프라이즈라고 판
단된다. 분기말 수주잔고는 35조원(수출 비중 74%, 스페인 미포함)으로 중장기 성장성에 대한 이견은 
없다. 불가피한 분기 실적 변동으로 동사의 미래 가치를 예단하는 것은 시기상조라는 판단이다.

Document 293: AI バブル測定器としての鉱工業生産（26 年９月）

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI バブル測定器としての鉱工業生産（26 年９月）.pdf
Report source: DLRI

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 30 日(金)

1/5 
AI バブル測定器としての鉱工業生産（26 年９月） 
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・筆者が日本経済と株式市場の行方を読む上で定点観測している日本の鉱工業生産は、2026年
９月に前月比＋2.2％となり、市場予想（同＋1.3％）を上回った。経産産業省の予測補正値
（同＋1.6％）に対しても上振れた。増産は３ヶ月ぶり。８月が速報段階の同▲1.7％から確
報で同▲2.4％へと下方修正されていたため、９月の増産で前月までの落ち込みを概ね取り戻
した格好となる。もっとも、生産水準は直近最高だった６月の104.6を上回るには至っておら
ず、数量ベースの生産活動は、経済産業省の判断通り「一進一退」となっている。

・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPYは先行き12ヶ月 155円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12月に 1.5％とした後、27年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FEDはFF金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

95

鉱工業生産指数
(備考）経済産業省により作成

### Page 2

2/5 
・増産となったのは８業種。上位３業種は自動車工業、生産用機械工業、汎用・業務機械工業
であった。反対に減産の寄与度が大きかった３業種は鉄鋼・非鉄金属、窯業・土石製品工
業、化学工業（除く無機・有機化学工業・医薬品）であった。７月に熊本で発生した地震に
よって８月までは自動車生産などに影響が出ていたが、その挽回生産が９月の数値を押し上
げる格好となった。

・10月初旬に実施された生産予測調査に基づけば、製造工業の生産計画は10月に前月比＋5.1％
となった後、11月は同＋0.8％と増産が見込まれている。経産省経済解析室が生産予測指数の
上振れバイアスを補正した10月の予測値は同＋3.9％とかなり強い。電子部品・デバイス工業
と生産用機械工業で大幅な増産が見込まれているほか、自動車の挽回生産も続く見込みであ
る。生産活動全体の方向感を決める輸送機械工業は９月（実績）に前月比＋5.5％となった
後、10月（計画）に同＋0.9％、11月に同＋4.3％となっている。日本や米国における自動車
販売台数が堅調に推移していることを踏まえると、基調的に弱含んでいく展開は想定しにく
い。また生産活動の先行指標として有用な製造業ＰＭＩが４月以降に有意な上昇を示してい
ることから判断すると、製造業全体としてはＡＩ・半導体需要が浸透する形で増勢が強まっ
ていくと期待される。10月製造業ＰＭＩは53.4と高水準を維持している。

・なお、鉱工業生産指数は生産の「数量」に重きを置いて生産高を計測する統計であり、「付
加価値」を直接捕捉する性格のものではない。例えば、高付加価値を狙った品質向上によっ
て生産数量・重量が減少する場合、統計上はそれを減産と見做し、付加価値を過小評価して
しまうこともある。したがって、鉱工業生産統計が示すほど日本経済（製造業）の付加価値

### Page 3

3/5 
創出力が停滞していない可能性を念頭に置く必要がある。事実、過去数年は付加価値の合計
である実質ＧＤＰと鉱工業生産指数の水準・方向感の乖離が大きくなっている。

・株式市場における重要度が高い電子部品・デバイス工業については、前月比＋3.6％の増産で
あった。地震の影響もあってか、ＩＣの増産に一服感がみられる一方、コンデンサや電子回
路基板などで増産の動きがみられる。生産用機械工業に分類される半導体製造装置は同＋
3.4％の増産となり、３ヶ月平均値でみても＋7.7％と力強い増産基調にある。生産予測調査
に目を向けると、電子部品・デバイス工業は10月に同＋16.6％、11月に同▲1.7％と均してみ
れば大幅な増産が計画されている。半導体製造装置が含まれる生産用機械工業は10月に同＋
15.5％と大幅増産となった後、11月は同▲5.7％と減産が見込まれているが、こちらも基調は
強い。個別企業の業績予想などから判断しても、先行きは半導体製造装置の増産がかなりの
高確度で期待できる。関連指標の機械受注統計における「電子計算機等」の受注額水準も鈍
化する気配に乏しい。この指標は日経平均株価との連動性がある。

### Page 4

4/5

・電子部品・デバイス工業の動向を仔細にみると、９月は出荷が前年比＋0.8％へと減速するな
か、在庫は同▲6.1％へとマイナス幅を拡大した。この結果、出荷・在庫バランス（両者の前
年比差分から算出）は＋6.9pとなり、３ヶ月平均でも＋7.0ptとはっきりとしたプラス領域を
維持した。引き続き、製品需給が引き締まる方向にあることが示唆されている。ここで電子
部品・デバイス工業の出荷・在庫バランスと、日経平均株価が長期的に連動性を有してきた
事実を再確認したい。株価指数において半導体を直接手掛ける企業の存在感は必ずしも大き
くはないものの、半導体製造装置、電子部品、化学品など「広義半導体」で見ればその存在
感は大きく、結果的に日本株全体のうねりを作り出す構図があると筆者は理解している。ち
なみに出荷在庫バランス株価は方向感こそ一致しているものの、両者のかい離は大きくなっ
ている。その要因として、数量ベースの出荷・在庫バランスには、メモリなど製品価格の高
騰が反映されないことが考えられる。

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。

・最後に先行きの出荷・在庫バランスを見極めるために在庫循環図の位置取りを確認すると、2 
025年は中心点付近で渦を描いてきたが、2026年入り後は右方向への動きが明確化しつつあ
る。過去の経験則に従えば、今後は右上領域（在庫増・出荷増）に向けて歩み出すと予想さ
れる。

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -32.53 điểm tương ứng -1.83%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Linh kiện ô tô, Dầu khí và Công nghệ là các nhóm ngành có m ức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -634 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã FPT, ACB và VPL; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng ở mức 
-609 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -5,829 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -1,462 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 722 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -5,278 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam đánh mất đà hồi phục của tuần trước, khi VN-Index quay đầu giảm khoảng 
1,8% về đóng cửa quanh 1.742 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm lùi sâu về sát 1.727 
điểm trước khi thu hẹp đà giảm. Dù mùa báo cáo KQKD quý 3 diễn ra tương đối tích cực, 
tuy nhiên các lo ngại về việc môi trường lãi suất cao vẫn duy trì trong bối cảnh căng thẳng 
Trung Đông leo thang đang đè nặng lên tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Kết quả kỳ họp của FED trong 
tuần qua nhìn chung phù hợp với kỳ vọng và không gây ra ảnh hưởng đáng kể. Điểm kém 
tích cực nhất là khối ngoại đã quay lại bán ròng với tổng giá trị khoảng 2.700 tỷ đồng 
trong tuần — đảo ngược diễn biến mua ròng cuối tuần trước, cho thấy đà rút vốn ngoại 
vẫn chưa thực sự dừng lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến giảm trên đồ thị tuần, nhưng chưa thoái lui quá 1 
nửa cây nến tăng của tuần trước, cùng thanh khoản được tiết giảm, hàm ý nhịp điều chỉnh 
vẫn trong phạm vi cho phép. Trên khung ngày, sau bốn phiên giằng co đầu tuần với thanh 
khoản thấp, chỉ số đã chịu áp lực sụt giảm mạnh hơn trong phiên cuối tuần, chớm xuống 
dưới đường tín hiệu chính giữa dải Bollinger, nhưng cho tín hiệu rút chân ngay sau đó, thể 
hiện áp lực bán tháo đã tạm thời được kiểm soát. Mặc dù rủi ro mở rộng thêm nhịp điều 
chỉnh vẫn còn để ngỏ, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn đang được bảo lưu, VNINDEX 
được kỳ vọng sẽ cho phản ứng hồi phục trở lại tại các điểm đỡ bên dưới.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1850 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1800 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1720 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1680 (+-15)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

Document 338: Cliff Notes_ the source and persistence of inflation (2026-11-13)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ the source and persistence of inflation (2026-11-13).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-13
Pages: 11
Status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: the source and persistence of inflation
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:16 October 30 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia this week, all eyes were on the September CPI. Headline inflation rose 0.8% (4.3%yr) in
the month, largely owing to a 9.4% jump in fuel prices and a seasonal surge in domestic and
international holiday travel & accommodation. The underlying inflation pulse also proved hotter than

anticipated for Q3 overall, up 1.0% (3.7%yr).

### Page 2

While some parts of the basket are seeing disinflation – housing in particular looks to be cooling faster
than expected as builders lure sales with discounts – other components are still showing persistence.
Highlighting this is market services ex volatiles, which the RBA watches closely as an indicator of price
pressures related to domestic capacity. In Q3, this metric rose 1.2% (3.2%yr), and the monthly detail
points to momentum holding up – in line with our expectations for another chunky read in Q4.

With inflation running well above the RBA’s August forecasts, and some of the energy risks flagged
then materialising, we continue to expect the Monetary Policy Board will deliver a 25bp rate hike to
4.85% in November. Beyond November, another rate hike in February is a risk, but is not currently part
of our base case.
Across the Tasman, the RBNZ this week surprised market expectations for a 25bp rate hike, deciding
instead to wait for further information before taking the next step with policy. The decision was made

4:3, with the Governor in the ‘on hold’ camp. A 25bp hike in December to 3.00% and further tightening
in 2027 (Westpac NZ forecasts a peak of 4.00% in September) seems probable; however, each
decision will depend on fuel prices, inflation expectations and evidence of second-round pricing
pressures, along with the evolution of global economic risks.
In the US, the FOMC also held rates steady at its October meeting. Based on prior comments by key
members, the decision was not a surprise, and neither were Governor Cook and Cleveland Fed

President Hammack’s dissents in favour of a 25bp hike. The Committee remains positive on economic
activity, characterising it in the statement as “expanding at a strong pace” (an upgrade from “solid” in
September). Chair Warsh also subsequently emphasised the breadth of economic growth outside of
tech-related investment (evinced by Q3 GDP the day after; see below).
The Committee refrained from spotlighting key price risks in the statement. However, inflation “remains

elevated” and the FOMC are determined to achieve a “timely” and sustainable return of annual inflation
to 2.0%yr after 6 years above target. This guidance and the “accumulation of more information will help
the Committee determine the policy stance” suggests another 25bp hike is likely in December.
Thereafter, the FOMC will remain attentive to any broadening in inflation and related risks through
2027; although, in the absence of another shock, we expect them to remain on hold.

The Q3 GDP report certainly supported Chair Warsh’s view on the breadth of growth, the 3.4%
annualised gain for GDP benefitting from a 3.8% annualised increase in household consumption,
12.7% growth in non-residential investment as well as a robust increase in government spending.
Notably though, the contribution from business investment was effectively offset by net exports’ drag
given most of the componentry related to AI infrastructure investment is imported. This is not to say that

US firms won’t benefit from the investment once complete, but simply that much of the initial return
goes to the foreign businesses who provide the necessary capital equipment.
On consumption, the monthly PCE data implies downside risks remain for the consumer, the increase
in nominal spending seen in the month requiring dissaving and, on a real basis, consumption flat in the
month following a 0.5% gain in August. Cost-of-living pressures and uncertainty over the outlook is

### Page 3

Related articles
expected to see household consumption growth average around trend over the coming year, albeit with
considerable volatility quarter to quarter. This should see PCE inflation slow back towards its 2.0%yr
medium-term target as the FOMC desires, from 3.6%yr and 3.1%yr for the headline and core
measures respectively as of September.
Across the Atlantic, the ECB also left its key policy rates unchanged in October. Of note from the
meeting communications, energy prices continue to pose upside risks for inflation, but also downside

risks for growth. "While the full effects of the energy shock are still unfolding, underlying inflation has
remained broadly stable so far and wages do not show a material response". "Financing conditions
have [also] tightened", "which is likely to weigh on inflation and growth". In the press conference,
President Lagarde noted that, between the October and December meetings, a significant amount of
new economic information will be released, allowing forecasts and risks assessments to be tested.

Based on the October meeting communications, we continue to expect a rate hike at the December
meeting but believe this will be prove to be the last hike of the cycle. Note though, the spectrum of risks
Europe faces is broad and skewed towards inflation in the near term, hence the Council’s guidance will
remain hawkish. President Lagarde also announced today that she will step down as ECB President in
February ahead of the scheduled conclusion of her term next October.

THE WEEKLY AGGREGATES REPORT

This report combines I/B/E/S's unrivaled historical earnings database, in depth coverage of Wall Street analysts' 
bottom-up corporate earnings estimates, and the analytic capabilities of the Proprietary Research group 
and desk-top solutions.

PROPRIETARY RESEARCH FROM LSEG October 30, 2026
WEEKLY AGGREGATES
ITEM DEFINITIONS AND METHODOLOGY
3© 2026 LSEG.  All marks herein are used under licence. TRPR_82222_875
General:
All aggregates are bottom-up aggregates calculated from analysts' consensus forecasts unless otherwise noted. Bottom-up
aggregates are weighted calculations using the I/B/E/S data from LSEG share class monitored or the official index weight of
the index-provider. Top-down forecasts are compilations of strategists' estimates. I/B/E/S universe calculations are based on

all companies within a given market monitored by LSEG.
Benchmark index calculations, including those for official local market indices, the S&P 500 Index, and the FTSE World Equity
Indices are calculated using official index constituents and weightings. All index composites are representative of current
index constituents. Index constituents and weightings are updated monthly during options expiration (the Thursday before the
third Friday of every month) for every index except the S&P 500, the constituents of which are current as of the date of

calculation.
PEIGBY refers to the Price Earnings Inverse Government Bond Yield ratio, which measures equity valuation with respect to
the inverse-yield on long-term government bonds (now known as the 'Fed' model). All PEIGBY ratios are I/B/E/S universe-
specific except for the US ratio, where it is based on the S&P 500 Index.
Methodology:
For US and selected Canadian aggregates, calendar-year aggregation is performed using the sum of four quarters' estimates

and a quarterly pro-ration is applied to non-December year end companies' quarterly EPS estimates. Outside of the US and
Canada, all company-level data are calendarized prior to aggregation using the Compustat method of calendarization.
Aggregation is performed using the annual consensus EPS estimate and a May roll-back is applied to December year-end
markets. Aggregates for Japan, where most companies report in March, use an August rollback and a November rollback is

used for Australia and New Zealand, where the fiscal year-end for most companies is in June.
All 12-month-forward data for ex-US markets are calculated using a pro-rata weighting to annual EPS forecasts. The
weightings applied are a daily calibration out of 365 days relative to each constituent's individual fiscal year-end and the
current date.
All non-surprise data, including aggregate forecast earnings, forecast growth, and forward-looking valuation calculations are

weighted according to the I/B/E/S share class monitored for I/B/E/S universes or the weightings assigned to each constituent
in a given index. Aggregated earnings calculations are the equivalent of net profit aggregates, as they are derived from the
EPS forecasts aggregated to the net income level by the appropriate share weighting.
Earnings Aggregation:
For 12-month-forward earnings, the 12-month-forward EPS is used in place of the calendarized EPS in the formulas below.

I/B/E/S universe EPS Aggregate = Index EPS Aggregate =
Growth Calculations:
All growth calculations are the weighted year over year growth in earnings for the calendarized fiscal year period. The
formulas below use index shares for index growth calculations.
Calendarized Earnings Growth = 12-month-forward Growth =
Valuation:
All valuation calculations are the weighted-average price-earnings ratio for the fiscal period in question. The formulas below

use index shares for index P/E calculations.
Calendarized P/E Aggregate = 12-month-forward P/E =
Revisions Momentum:
Unless otherwise indicated, analysts' revisions momentum is a simple aggregation of the total number of estimates raised
divided by the total number of estimates lowered. Thus, any revisions ratio less than 1 indicates negative revisions
momentum while a ratio greater than 1 indicates positive revisions momentum, with 1 marking parity.

The Global Aggregates from LSEG product contains annual aggregates for universes included in this publication, including
supplemental aggregate data of over 100 items as well as history dating back to 1985 for North America and to 1987 for other
markets. To learn more about this product, contact your LSEG sales representative.
FTSE and the FTSE World Equity Indices are owned by FTSE International Limited.

### Page 1

### Page 2

Westpac New Zealand Economics
2
Michael Gordon, Senior Economist | +64 9 336 5670 | michael.gordon@westpac.co.nz      Connect with us
Key points – September quarter 2026
• The September quarter surveys pointed to ongoing softness in labour market conditions. The unemployment rate rose from 5.6% to 5.7%, continuing an
almost five-year run of increases. The unemployment rate is now at its highest level since September 2015.
• There were mixed messages in terms of jobs growth: the Household Labour Force Survey reported a 0.1% fall in employment, while the employer-

focused Quarterly Employment Survey suggested a modest rise. The Monthly Employment Indicator had also pointed to a small rise in jobs.
• The fall in HLFS employment was accompanied by a fall in the participation rate. These co-movements essentially reversed the surprisingly large
increases that we saw in the June quarter, which we had suggested at the time were more likely due to sampling error in the survey rather than a
genuine surge.
• We generally recommend focusing on the unemployment rate, which is less vulnerable to sampling errors – and the message here is one of softness. Our

best interpretation is that, to the extent that jobs are growing, it’s not fast enough to absorb the growth in the population.
• While the economy has returned to growth over the last year or so, hiring activity remains weak. This may be an ongoing legacy of employment trends in
previous years, when firms were relatively reluctant to lay people off during the slowdown, which then potentially left them overstaffed even as activity
started to pick up again. It’s not clear how long this effect can persist, though.

• Wage growth remains muted. Although cost-of-living pressures have increased on workers, the existing degree of spare capacity in the labour market
doesn’t give them much leverage to negotiate larger pay increases.
• Overall, we judge these results to be weaker than the RBNZ expected, in particular the rise in the unemployment rate. This data supports the views of
those on the policy committee who were less convinced in October about the potential for second-round inflation pressures in the economy.

### Page 3

Westpac New Zealand Economics
3
Employment
• The number of people employed fell by 0.1% in the September quarter, according to the
Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS). Previous quarters were revised down a little,
reflecting downward revisions to the working-age population estimates. Annual employment
growth slowed from 0.9% to 0.7%.
• This was substantially softer than our forecast of a 0.5% rise for the quarter. Our forecast
was based on the Monthly Employment Indicator (MEI), a comprehensive measure that is

drawn from income tax data. While there are some differences in coverage for these two
measures, any significant divergence between the two is most likely to reflect sampling error
in the HLFS.
• Meanwhile, the employer-focused Quarterly Employment Survey (QES) recorded a 0.1% rise
in the number of filled jobs and a 0.6% rise in hours paid. (This survey is of course vulnerable
to sampling error as well.)
• We’re most inclined towards the MEI measure of employment, given the breadth of its

coverage. But our best interpretation is that, to the extent that jobs are growing, it’s not
yet fast enough to absorb the growth in the adult population – hence the further rise in the
unemployment rate.

### Page 4

Westpac New Zealand Economics
4
Labour force participation
• The surprising fall in HLFS employment was accompanied by a drop in the participation rate
from 70.6% to 70.4%.
• These co-movements are effectively a reversal of the surprisingly large increases that we
saw in the June quarter, which we noted at the time were suggestive of sampling error in the
survey. Any such errors largely wash out when it comes to the unemployment rate, however,
which is why we recommend focusing on this measure.

• Setting aside the usual quarterly volatility, labour force participation remains caught
between two competing forces. One is cyclical weakness, especially among younger people,
who are finding it difficult to break into a “low-fire, low-hire” labour market. The teen
unemployment rate of around 25% is a record high going back to the start of the survey
in 1986.
• The other force is rising participation among the over-60s (who also make up a growing

share of the population). Kiwis have had a growing propensity to work beyond the NZ Super
age, as there is no penalty for doing so. It’s unclear to what extent this can be considered
an increase in the pool of available workers: older workers tend to have low mobility
between jobs, and if they find themselves out of work are more likely to leave the labour
force altogether.

### Page 5

Westpac New Zealand Economics
5
Unemployment and underutilisation
• The unemployment rate rose from 5.6% to 5.7% in the September quarter. This was the
highest rate since September 2015.
• While there are other indicators of spare capacity in the labour market, we’re not convinced
that there is additional information to draw from them this time.
• The broader underutilisation measure, which includes discouraged jobseekers and part-
time workers wanting more hours, was steady at 13.8% after having risen sharply from

12.9% last quarter. This measure is both higher (by definition) and more variable than the
unemployment rate, but tells a similar story in that it’s around an 11-year high.
• There was a marked 1.6% fall in the number of hours worked (although ‘usual’ hours were
down just 0.4%). However, we suspect this was largely driven by Matariki – a floating holiday
that won’t be captured in the seasonal adjustment.
• This may also account for the 2.5% rise in part-time employment, against a 0.7% fall in

full-time employment. Some workers may have been pushed into the part-time category
(less than 30 hours a week) simply by virtue of only working four days during the
survey week.

### Page 6

Westpac New Zealand Economics
6
Hiring
• Business confidence surveys have shown a lift in hiring intentions in recent months, as firms
have shaken off the initial shock of the Iran conflict and the surge in fuel prices.
• However, those stated intentions aren’t obviously turning into reality yet. Job advertisements
were down about 1% for the September quarter in seasonally adjusted terms, and they
remain well below pre-Covid levels. Job ads will capture ‘churn’ within existing roles as well

as new job creation, but both of these would be associated with a strong labour market.
• With the working-age population growing at the same time, the result was a modest fall in
the vacancy rate, one of the RBNZ’s highest-ranked measures of labour market tightness.
• We estimate that there are around 1.9 people unemployed for every job vacancy, up from
around 1.8 people this time last year.
• Firms have told us that they were reluctant to lay off workers during the economic downturn

in 2023-24, as they were wary of the extreme labour shortages that had developed in the
previous boom years. As a result, they may find themselves still overstaffed even as demand
has started to pick up again, and haven’t needed to return to hiring yet.

### Page 7

Westpac New Zealand Economics
7
Population and migration
• The working-age population grew by around 1.0% over the last year, compared to a peak of
3.3% growth over 2023. Jobs growth (based on the less volatile MEI measure) is still lagging
behind population growth on an annual basis.
• The pool of potential workers is generally becoming older, with the Baby Boomers
increasingly reaching pension age and beyond, and with migrant arrivals tending to be an

older cohort than we saw pre-Covid – more in their 30s and 40s than their 20s.
• Net migration flows have started to pick up from their lows since late 2025. Departures of
New Zealanders have come down from their highs, though they remain above pre-Covid
levels, as the relative appeal of the Australian jobs market has narrowed.
• Foreign arrivals have picked up to some degree, although the growth appears to have been
mostly driven by students rather than those on work visas.

### Page 8

Westpac New Zealand Economics
8
Sectoral and regional trends
• Jobs growth has been relatively strong in government-connected sectors such as healthcare
and education, as well as in local and central government administration.
• Mining and utilities have seen strong growth in percentage terms, but neither of these
sectors are big employers in absolute terms.
• The construction sector is now starting to see a lift in employment, after a steep decline

over 2024-25. Residential building consents have risen strongly over the last year or so,
pointing to a growing pipeline of work for the homebuilding industry.
• Sectors that are connected to international tourism, such as transport and recreational
services, had previously seen strong employment growth, but this appears to have flattened
out in recent months.
• Employment trends have generally been more positive in the South Island than the North,

which likely reflects the greater influence of the agricultural sector – while not a large
employer in itself, farmers have benefited from high export prices and their spending has
flowed into other sectors.

### Page 9

Westpac New Zealand Economics
9
Wages
• The Labour Cost Index (LCI) rose by 0.5% in the September quarter. Annual growth
remained around the lowest since 2021.
• While the overall result was in line with our forecast, the details were a little weaker than
we expected, with less contribution from private sector wages (up 0.5%) and a surprisingly
large 0.7% increase in the public sector.
• The distribution of pay rates has returned to pre-Covid trends. Only 44% of roles saw a

pay rise over the last year; of those roles that did see an increase, the average size has
converged on the 2-3% range.
• The LCI is not a measure of what workers actually receive – it reflects the cost to businesses
for a given unit of labour – and hence shouldn’t be compared to the inflation rate. A more
suitable measure is the analytical unadjusted LCI, which includes pay rises that are related
to productivity gains. This measure slowed from 3.1% to 2.9% on an annual basis, the lowest

since March 2021.
• There was a marked pickup in the proportion of firms reporting cost-of-living adjustments as
the main reason for pay increases. However, the size of those increases varied greatly, with
many of them below the rate of CPI inflation.
• This seems to reflect a shift in the justification for pay rises, rather than an overall increase in
wage pressures – firms are more likely to cite cost-of-living adjustments because they’re less
likely to cite demand-side pressures.

### Page 10

Westpac New Zealand Economics
10
International comparisons
• New Zealand’s unemployment rate is the 18th lowest in the OECD (out of 38 countries). This
ranking has been broadly unchanged over the last year or so.
• Notably, New Zealand’s unemployment rate is substantially above Australia’s, whereas
historically it has been lower on average. This shift in relative job opportunities has been a
key attractor for New Zealanders over the last couple of years – as it was in the early 2010s

when the Australian economy was benefiting from a boom in mining construction.
• However, that gap between unemployment rates is no longer widening, with the Australian
jobs market starting to soften this year as the RBA has raised interest rates to rein in inflation
(which was already running too high before the Iran conflict and the surge in oil prices). We
expect Australia’s unemployment to rise further over the year ahead.
• New Zealand has the 7th highest employment rate in the OECD (down from 6th last quarter).

We tend to have high rates of labour force participation for 15-64 year olds, and particularly
high participation for the over-65s, as the lack of means testing for NZ Superannuation
means there is no financial disincentive to working beyond pension age.

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 28 日

1/4 
RBNZ の予想外の利上げ見送りで、NZ ドル相場は一段と弱含むか 
～追加利上げの時期や総選挙を巡る不透明感が重なり、豪ドルとの対照的な動きが続くか～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ ニュージーランド準備銀行（RBNZ）は、10 月 27～28 日に開催した定例理事会において、政
策金利を2.75％に据え置くことを決定した。RBNZ は７月に約３年ぶりの利上げに舵を切り、
９月にも追加利上げを実施するなど金融引き締めを強めてきた。このため、今回の据え置き
は３会合ぶりであり、金融市場の事前予想に反する決定となった。 
○ ７-９月のインフレ率は前年比＋3.7％と前期から鈍化したものの、コアインフレ率は加速す
るなど、インフレ圧力は依然として根強いことが確認された。このため、金融市場において
は RBNZ が今回利上げに動くことを織り込む動きが広がりをみせていた。こうしたなか、 今回
の決定は４対３の僅差となったほか、声明文や総裁会見でも追加利上げの必要性が示された
ものの、そのタイミングについては不透明感が残る。 
○ また、11 月７日の総選挙を控え、RBNZ の政策目標の見直しが争点となっていることも、 先行
きの政策運営を巡る不透明感につながっている。金融市場では、予想外の金利据え置きに対
する 「失望」 に加え、 金融政策が後手に回るとの警戒感もＮＺドル相場の重しとなっている。
当面はＮＺドル相場の上値が重い展開が続くと見込まれ、オセアニア通貨のなかでも豪ドル
とＮＺドルが対照的な動きをみせる可能性がある。

【RBNZは事前予想に反して、10 月の定例会合で３会合ぶりに政策金利を据え置く決定】 
ニュージーランド準備銀行（RBNZ）は、10 月 27～28 日に開催した定例理事会において、政策金
利であるオフィシャル・キャッシュ・レート（ＯＣＲ）を2.75％に据え置くことを決定した。ニュ
ージーランドでは、2025 年以降にインフレ率が加速し、RBNZ の定める目標レンジ（１～３％）の
上限を上回る伸びで推移している。 さらに、 イラン情勢の悪化を受けた原油価格の上昇がエネルギ
ーインフレを招いたことから、RBNZ は７月の定例会合で約３年ぶりの利上げに舵を切り、 ９月会合
でも追加利上げに動くなど、金融引き締めを強化してきた（注１）。このため、RBNZ による金利据

注１ ９月２日付レポート「RBNZ は緩やかな引き締め示唆､NZ ドル相場の行方はどうなる？」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/654752.html）

### Page 2

2/4 
え置きは３会合ぶりとなる。 
RBNZ のブレマン総裁は、 ９月の定例会合後の記者会見において、 追加利上げに含みを持たせる考
えを示していた。一方で、予め決められた方針に固執せず、追加利上げのタイミングは極めて不確
実と述べるとともに、これまでの利上げの効果などを見極める考えをみせた。声明文でも、２会合
連続の利上げ実施を受けて、 先行きは大幅利上げに動く必要性は低下したとの認識を示すなど、「タ
カ派」姿勢が後退している様子がうかがえた。こうした姿勢の変化もあり、ＲＢＮＺによる断続的
な利上げ実施にもかかわらず、その後のＮＺドル相場は調整の動きを強めた。 
同国では、11 月７日に議会（代議院：一院制）の総選挙が実施されるが、RBNZ のマンデート（政
策目標）が争点のひとつとなっていることも、ＮＺドル相場が調整の動きを強める一因となった。
最大野党の左派・労働党を中心とする政
党連合は、マンデートに持続可能な雇用
の最大化を再び加えることを公約に掲げ
ている。世論調査では、右派の国民党を
中心とする与党連合、左派連合の競り合
いが続いており、新興政党・オポチュニ
ティ党の動向が注目されている（注２）。
足元の雇用環境をみると、 直近４-６月の
失業率が 10 年ぶりの高水準となってお
り（図１）、 仮にマンデートが変更されれ
ば、利上げのハードルが高まる可能性が
ある。こうした事情もＮＺドル相場の重しになったと考えられる。 
一方で、RBNZ のブレマン総裁は、９月の定例会合後の記者会見において、選挙結果が政策運営に
影響を与えることはないとの見方を示し
た。このため、金融市場では今回の定例
会合を占ううえで、物価動向に注目した。
７-９月のインフレ率は前年比＋3.7％と
５四半期連続で目標レンジを上回ったも
のの、 前期 （同＋4.1％） から鈍化し、RBNZ
が９月の定例会合で示した見通し（前年
比＋3.9％）も下回った（図２） 。コアイ
ンフレ率は前年比＋ 2.9％と目標レンジ
内で推移しているものの、前期（同＋
2.6％） から加速し、７四半期ぶりの高い

3/4 
伸びとなるなど、 インフレ圧力が高まっている様子が確認された。このため、 金融市場ではRBNZ が
追加利上げに動くとの見方が広がったものの、予想に反して政策金利を据え置いた格好となった。

【僅差での据え置き決定、市場の「失望」を反映してNZドル相場は上値の重い展開が続くか】 
会合後に公表された声明文では、今回の決定について「４（据え置き）対３（25bp の利上げ）の
賛成多数で金利据え置きを決定した」とした。足元の物価動向について「想定を下回った」 、 「賃金
上昇が抑制されており、 中期的なインフレ期待は安定している」 とする一方、 「コアインフレ率は目
標レンジの上限近くに加速しており、物価上昇の動きが広範に 及びつつある」との見方を示した。
先行きについては「世界的な情勢が短期的にインフレ圧力を一段と強めている」として、その要因
に「原油高によるエネルギー価格の上昇と通貨ＮＺドル安」 を挙げた。 そして、 「10-12 月のインフ
レ率は＋4.4％に加速が見込まれる」としつつ、 「原油や石油製品価格の変動が激しく、インフレ見
通しは不透明」との見方を示した。 
実体経済については「回復が続いているものの、状況には依然ばらつきがある」との見方を示し
た。具体的には「外需関連産業では、商品市況の上昇、世界的な需要の堅調さ、外国人観光客数の
増加の恩恵を受けている」一方、 「内需関連産業は低調に推移している」とした。一方で、 「オーク
ランドを中心に住宅投資の回復の兆しがでている」とした。 
政策運営については「物価に対する一時的な要因を差し引いた上で、 経済に不必要な不安定を起
こすことを回避しつつ、中期的にインフレ率を目標レンジの中央（２％）に戻すことに注力してい
る」とあらためて強調した。 そのうえで、 「家計消費が弱く、 一部の企業ではコスト上昇の転嫁が制
限されている」 としつつ、 「インフレ率が高止まりすれば、 コスト上昇がインフレ期待や価格や賃金
に波及してインフレがより持続的なものとなるリスクが高まる」との見方を示した。 
世界経済については「堅調に推移しているものの、地政学リスクやエネルギーインフレ、金融市
場のボラティリティの高まり、ＡＩ（人工知能）関連投資の急増などを背景に、その脆弱性は高ま
っている」との見方を示した。一方で、同国経済について「回復の動きが予想以上に力強くなる可
能性はある一方、世界経済の悪化、外需の好調さが雇用や所得、家計消費に波及しなければ勢いを
失う可能性がある」との認識を示した。そのうえで、今回の決定について「政策委員は全体的なイ
ンフレ見通しでは一致したが、個々のリスクに対する見方や金融政策の対応時期に関する意見は割
れた」、先行きについても「追加利上げが必要となる可能性は高いとの見方で一致したものの、 その
タイミングは経済見通し次第」として、追加利上げに含みを持たせる考えを示した。 
会合後に記者会見に臨んだブレマン総裁は、今回の協議について「景気動向を勘案すれば、より
抑制的な政策が必要になることで合意した」 、 「ＮＺドル安が続けば輸入インフレを助長する可能性
がある」との認識が共有されたとした。先行きについては「さらなる引き締めが必要となり、状況
によっては引き締め的な領域に入る可能性がある」 、 「今後の会合で追加利上げを行う可能性が高い」
との認識を示した。そして、 「ＯＣＲの道筋が９月会合の想定を上回る可能性についても協議した」
としつつ、 「中立金利の見通しには幅があり、 常に不確実性がある」 として、 具体的な水準への言及
は避けた。一方で、 今回の決定については「経済見通しに基づいた決定である」 としたうえで、 「イ

### Page 4

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
4/4 
ンフレ率を目標レンジに戻すための条件は依然として良好」 、 「賃金上昇率は目標とするインフレ動
向に見合う水準を下回っている」との認識を示した。 
前述したように、金融市場においては今回の利上げを織り込む動きが広がっていたため、予想外
の金利据え置き決定に対しては『失望』
にも似た反応がみられ、 ＮＺドル相場は
調整の動きを強めている （図３） 。背景に
は、 総選挙の行方が不透明であることに
加え、その後の 12 月に開催される次回
の定例会合において、 追加利上げが実施
されるか否かについても不透明感が残
ることがある。一部では、金融政策が後
手に回る（behind the curve）との警戒
感が強まっていることも影響している
と考えられる。このため、当面のＮＺド
ル相場は弱含む展開が続くと予想され、 オセアニア通貨では豪ドルが強含む一方、ＮＺドルは上値
の重い展開が続くなど、両通貨が対照的な動きをみせる可能性が高まっている。
